# 百川接口清单

来源：http://10.10.0.150/swagger.json

版本：1.11.3；路径：492；操作：551；服务分类：39。

由 gRPC 服务定义的 google.api.http 注解自动生成；server-streaming 接口以 SSE（text/event-stream）逐帧推送，多路聚合订阅见 /mapi/v1/streaming。

说明：仅解析接口定义，未执行控制接口。完整模型与引用见 swagger原始文档.json。


### GET /mapi/v1/acm/events:watch

订阅事件流（server-streaming）



操作标识：AddonManagementService_SubscribeEvents

**参数**

```json
[
  {
    "name": "pool",
    "description": "插件池过滤（缺省=全部）",
    "in": "query",
    "required": false,
    "type": "string"
  },
  {
    "name": "types",
    "description": "事件类型过滤（空=全部）\n\n - EVENT_TYPE_UNKNOWN: 未知\n - EVENT_TYPE_HOST_STARTED: addon-host 进程启动\n - EVENT_TYPE_ADDON_STATE_CHANGED: 插件状态变更（注：心跳事件仅用于判死，不进入事件记录与订阅流）\n - EVENT_TYPE_ADDON_HEALTH_CHANGED: 插件健康变更\n - EVENT_TYPE_CONTRACT_REGISTERED: 插件注册契约\n - EVENT_TYPE_CONFIG_CHANGED: 插件回传有效配置\n - EVENT_TYPE_HOST_EXITED: 宿主进程退出（manager 合成）\n - EVENT_TYPE_POOL_ACTION: 插件池操作（manager 合成：start/stop/restart/apply）",
    "in": "query",
    "required": false,
    "type": "array",
    "items": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "EVENT_TYPE_UNKNOWN",
        "EVENT_TYPE_HOST_STARTED",
        "EVENT_TYPE_ADDON_STATE_CHANGED",
        "EVENT_TYPE_ADDON_HEALTH_CHANGED",
        "EVENT_TYPE_CONTRACT_REGISTERED",
        "EVENT_TYPE_CONFIG_CHANGED",
        "EVENT_TYPE_HOST_EXITED",
        "EVENT_TYPE_POOL_ACTION"
      ]
    },
    "collectionFormat": "multi"
  }
]
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "200": {
    "description": "A successful response.(streaming responses)",
    "schema": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "result": {
          "$ref": "#/definitions/acmAddonEvent"
        },
        "error": {
          "$ref": "#/definitions/rpcStatus"
        }
      },
      "title": "Stream result of acmAddonEvent"
    }
  },
  "default": {
    "description": "An unexpected error response.",
    "schema": {
      "$ref": "#/definitions/rpcStatus"
    }
  }
}
```

### GET /mapi/v1/cs-kv/databases/{database}/stores/{store}/keys

列举 store 内的 key（字典序；skip/take 分页）



操作标识：ClientDataStorageService_ListKeys

**参数**

```json
[
  {
    "name": "database",
    "description": "database 名",
    "in": "path",
    "required": true,
    "type": "string"
  },
  {
    "name": "store",
    "description": "store 名",
    "in": "path",
    "required": true,
    "type": "string"
  },
  {
    "name": "prefix",
    "description": "key 前缀过滤（不传表示不过滤）",
    "in": "query",
    "required": false,
    "type": "string"
  },
  {
    "name": "skip",
    "description": "跳过的条目数（不传为 0）",
    "in": "query",
    "required": false,
    "type": "integer",
    "format": "int32"
  },
  {
    "name": "take",
    "description": "返回的最大条目数（默认 100，最大 1000，超上限按上限截断；\ninclude_values=true 时上限收紧为 100，控制单页响应体积）",
    "in": "query",
    "required": false,
    "type": "integer",
    "format": "int32"
  },
  {
    "name": "includeValues",
    "description": "是否在响应条目中携带 value 与 updated_at",
    "in": "query",
    "required": false,
    "type": "boolean"
  }
]
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "200": {
    "description": "A successful response.",
    "schema": {
      "$ref": "#/definitions/datacenterListClientDataKeysResponse"
    }
  },
  "default": {
    "description": "An unexpected error response.",
    "schema": {
      "$ref": "#/definitions/rpcStatus"
    }
  }
}
```

### PUT /mapi/v1/cs-kv/databases/{database}/stores/{store}/values/{key}

写入单条数据（upsert；store 不存在时隐式创建）
REST body 为 {"value": <任意 JSON>}（body:"*"：Envoy transcoder 对裸数字绑定 Value 字段有解析缺陷，
包装一层后全类型可用）



操作标识：ClientDataStorageService_PutValue

**参数**

```json
[
  {
    "name": "database",
    "description": "database 名",
    "in": "path",
    "required": true,
    "type": "string"
  },
  {
    "name": "store",
    "description": "store 名",
    "in": "path",
    "required": true,
    "type": "string"
  },
  {
    "name": "key",
    "description": "条目 key",
    "in": "path",
    "required": true,
    "type": "string"
  },
  {
    "name": "body",
    "in": "body",
    "required": true,
    "schema": {
      "$ref": "#/definitions/ClientDataStorageServicePutValueBody"
    }
  }
]
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "200": {
    "description": "A successful response.",
    "schema": {
      "$ref": "#/definitions/commonNothing"
    }
  },
  "default": {
    "description": "An unexpected error response.",
    "schema": {
      "$ref": "#/definitions/rpcStatus"
    }
  }
}
```

### POST /mapi/v1/cs-kv/databases/{database}/stores/{store}/values/{key}:compare-and-swap

原子比较替换：当前值与 expected_value 相等时写入 new_value 并返回 applied=true；
不相等时不做修改，返回 applied=false 及当前条目。
expected_value 不设置表示预期 key 不存在（不存在则创建，即 create-if-absent）



操作标识：ClientDataStorageService_CompareAndSwapValue

**参数**

```json
[
  {
    "name": "database",
```

### GET /mapi/v1/cs-kv/databases/{database}/stores/{store}/values/{key}:fetch

按 JSON Pointer（RFC 6901，如 /a/b/0；REST 中以 path 查询参数传递）读取条目值的子值，
path 为空串表示取整个值。
key 不存在或 path 在值上无法解析（字段缺失/类型不匹配/数组越界）返回 NOT_FOUND；
path 语法非法返回 INVALID_ARGUMENT



操作标识：ClientDataStorageService_FetchValue

**参数**

```json
[
  {
    "name": "database",
    "description": "database 名",
    "in": "path",
    "required": true,
    "type": "string"
  },
  {
    "name": "store",
    "description": "store 名",
    "in": "path",
    "required": true,
    "type": "string"
  },
  {
    "name": "key",
    "description": "条目 key",
    "in": "path",
    "required": true,
    "type": "string"
  },
  {
    "name": "path",
    "description": "JSON Pointer（RFC 6901）路径，如 /a/b/0；空串表示取整个值",
    "in": "query",
    "required": false,
    "type": "string"
  }
]
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "200": {
    "description": "A successful response.",
    "schema": {
      "$ref": "#/definitions/datacenterFetchClientDataValueResponse"
    }
  },
  "default": {
    "description": "An unexpected error response.",
    "schema": {
      "$ref": "#/definitions/rpcStatus"
    }
  }
}
```

```json
    "required": false,

    "format": "int64"
  }
]
```

```json
    "description": "对象字典索引（如 0x1018）",
    "in": "path",
    "required": true,
    "type": "integer",
    "format": "int64"
  },
  {
    "name": "subIndex",
    "description": "子索引",
    "in": "path",
    "required": true,
    "type": "integer",
    "format": "int64"
  },
  {
    "name": "timeoutMs",
    "description": "调用超时（毫秒），0 表示默认 5s",
    "in": "query",
    "required": false,
    "type": "string",
    "format": "int64"
  }
]
```

**响应**

## OSSettingService

### GET /mapi/v1/device/os/bundles

查询已安装的 deb 包列表



操作标识：OSSettingService_QueryBundles

**参数**

```json
[
  {
    "name": "bundleName",
    "description": "按名称模糊查询（可选）",
    "in": "query",
    "required": false,
    "type": "string"
  },
  {
    "name": "version",
    "description": "按版本查询（可选，支持精确匹配）",
    "in": "query",
    "required": false,
    "type": "string"
  },
  {
    "name": "type",
    "description": "按类型筛选（可选，可多选）\n\n - BUNDLE_TYPE_UNKNOWN: 未知类型\n - BUNDLE_TYPE_OS_PKG: 操作系统应用\n - BUNDLE_TYPE_CORE_SERVICE: 核心服务\n - BUNDLE_TYPE_CORE_COMPONENT: 核心组件\n - BUNDLE_TYPE_CORE_ADDON: 核心插件\n - BUNDLE_TYPE_BTX_ADDON: 行为树组件",
    "in": "query",
    "required": false,
    "type": "array",
    "items": {
      "type": "string",
      "enum": [
        "BUNDLE_TYPE_UNKNOWN",
        "BUNDLE_TYPE_OS_PKG",
        "BUNDLE_TYPE_CORE_SERVICE",
        "BUNDLE_TYPE_CORE_COMPONENT",
        "BUNDLE_TYPE_CORE_ADDON",
        "BUNDLE_TYPE_BTX_ADDON"
      ]
    },
    "collectionFormat": "multi"
  },
  {
    "name": "skip",
    "description": "跳过数量",
    "in": "query",
    "required": false,
    "type": "integer",
    "format": "int32"
  },
  {
    "name": "take",
    "description": "返回数量",
    "in": "query",
    "required": false,
    "type": "integer",
    "format": "int32"
  }
]
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "200": {
    "description": "A successful response.",
    "schema": {
      "$ref": "#/definitions/osalQueryBundlesResponse"
    }
  },
  "default": {
    "description": "An unexpected error response.",
    "schema": {
      "$ref": "#/definitions/rpcStatus"
    }
  }
}
```

```json
    "format": "int64"

  {

    "in": "query",
    "required": false,
    "type": "array",
    "items": {
      "type": "integer",
      "format": "int64"
    },
    "collectionFormat": "multi"
  }
]
```

```json
    }
  }
}
```

## NotificationCenterService

### GET /mapi/v1/notifications

获取当前活跃通知的全量快照（unary，一次性查询）。
适用于页面初始化拉取、断流期间补偿查询；返回内容与新订阅首帧一致。



操作标识：NotificationCenterService_GetActiveNotifications

**响应**

```json
{
  "200": {
    "description": "A successful response.",
    "schema": {
      "$ref": "#/definitions/miscActiveNotifications"
    }
  },
  "default": {
    "description": "An unexpected error response.",
    "schema": {
      "$ref": "#/definitions/rpcStatus"
    }
  }
}
```

### POST /mapi/v1/notifications/actions

提交用户动作：应答询问 / 关闭提示 / 取消进度任务（unary）。
硬校验（任一不满足则 accepted=false，零分发零回推）：
  - target_id 必须命中 center 活跃缓存（含终态驻留条目）
  - action 与条目 kind 匹配（见 NotificationAction）
  - CANCEL 时条目须 cancellable
校验通过后：条目迁移至终态（驻留 1s 后移除）、快照重推、
动作经 /notification/resolved 回推给该通知的 source 生产者。



操作标识：NotificationCenterService_SubmitNotificationAction

**参数**

```json
[
  {
    "name": "body",
    "in": "body",
    "required": true,
    "schema": {
      "$ref": "#/definitions/miscSubmitNotificationActionRequest"
    }
  }
]
```

**响应**

```json
{
  "200": {
    "description": "A successful response.",
    "schema": {
      "$ref": "#/definitions/miscSubmitNotificationActionResponse"
    }
  },
  "default": {
    "description": "An unexpected error response.",
    "schema": {
      "$ref": "#/definitions/rpcStatus"
    }
  }
}
```

### GET /mapi/v1/notifications/watch

订阅活跃通知（server-streaming）。
流建立时立即推送一帧当前缓存的全量快照（断联重连自动补发）；
此后活跃集合任何 增/删/内容覆盖/状态迁移 即推送新的全量快照；
generation 单调递增，跳号即重建订阅。
快照内同时包含"活跃中"与"终态 1s 驻留中"的条目，由 state 区分。



操作标识：NotificationCenterService_WatchActiveNotifications

**响应**

```json
{
  "200": {
    "description": "A successful response.(streaming responses)",
    "schema": {
      "type": "object",
      "properties": {
        "result": {
          "$ref": "#/definitions/miscActiveNotifications"
        },
        "error": {
          "$ref": "#/definitions/rpcStatus"
        }
      },
      "title": "Stream result of miscActiveNotifications"
    }
  },
  "default": {
    "description": "An unexpected error response.",
    "schema": {
      "$ref": "#/definitions/rpcStatus"
    }
  }
}
```

### PUT /mapi/v1/ui-home-page/packages/{pageId}/state

更新组件状态对象（补丁语义：仅应用请求中显式给出的字段，未给出的字段保持不变；
options 做 key 级合并而非整体替换，value 为 null 表示删除该 key）
约束：is_automation_home 置 true 时组件须处于启用状态（含本次同 patch 的 enabled），否则返回 INVALID_ARGUMENT；
同一时刻全部组件中最多一个 is_automation_home 为 true（服务端置 true 时自动清除其他组件的该标记）



操作标识：UiPagePackageManagementService_UpdateUiPagePackageState

**参数**

```json
[
  {
    "name": "pageId",
    "description": "组件id（必填）",
    "in": "path",
    "required": true,
    "type": "string"
  },
  {
    "name": "body",
    "in": "body",
    "required": true,
    "schema": {
      "$ref": "#/definitions/UiPagePackageManagementServiceUpdateUiPagePackageStateBody"
    }
  }
]
```

**响应**
